===== PDF PAGE 7 =====
v
Digitalisasi telah merubah proses dan cara hidup dalam masyarakat 
secara luas, baik dari hal sederhana hingga kompleks. Adaptasi terhadap 
perkembangan teknologi diperlukan untuk meningkatkan kualitas hidup 
dalam menghadapi berbagai tantangan global. Untuk itu diperlukan indikator-
indikator statistik sebagai alat ukur kemajuan dan perencanaan menyambut 
transformasi teknologi di masa depan. 
Publikasi Statistik Telekomunikasi Indonesia 2024 adalah publikasi 
tahunan yang diterbitkan Badan Pusat Statistik (BPS). Data yang disajikan 
merupakan hasil dari Survei Sosial Ekonomi Nasional (Susenas) dan Survei 
Potensi Desa (Podes) yang dilaksanakan oleh BPS serta kompilasi produk data 
administrasi dari berbagai instansi terkait. Publikasi ini memuat indikator 
yang memberikan gambaran terkini penetrasi teknologi informasi dan 
komunikasi dalam masyarakat, serta informasi aktivitas di berbagai bidang 
telekomunikasi di Indonesia, seperti jaringan dan jasa telekomunikasi.  
Kami mengucapkan terima kasih dan mengapresiasi semua pihak yang 
telah mendukung terwujudnya publikasi ini. Kami berharap melalui publikasi 
ini dapat memberikan manfaat kepada para pengguna serta mendukung 
perkembangan teknologi informasi dan komunikasi di Indonesia.
Jakarta, Agustus 2025
Kepala Badan Pusat Statistik
Amalia Adininggar Widyasanti
KATA
PENGANTAR
https://www.bps.go.id


===== PDF PAGE 8 =====
vi
Jakarta, August 2025
Chief Statistician
Amalia Adininggar Widyasanti
PREF ACE
Digitalization has profoundly reshaped societal processes and lifestyles, 
from simple to complex aspects of daily life. Adapting to technological 
advancements is crucial to improving quality of life and addressing global 
challenges. In this context, statistical indicators serve as essential tools for 
monitoring progress and guiding development planning.
Indonesia Telecommunication Statistics 2024 is an annual publication 
issued 
by Statistics Indonesia ( BPS). The data presented are derived from 
the National Socio-Economic Survey ( Susenas), the Village Potential Survey 
(PODES), both conducted by BPS, as well as compilations of administrative data 
from 
various relevant institutions. This publication provides key indicators that 
reflect the current state of information and communication technology (ICT) 
penetration in society, 
along with insights into various telecommunications 
activities in Indonesia, including infrastructure and service-related aspects, in 
anticipation of future technological transformation.
We extend 
our sincere appreciation to all parties who have contributed 
to the realization of this publication. We hope this publication proves valuable 
to users and supports the advancement of information and communication 
technology in Indonesia.
https://www.bps.go.id

National Socio-Economic Survey (SUSENAS) includes individuals aged 5 years and older.

for internet access is the past three months prior to the enumeration.

===== PDF PAGE 27 =====
xxv
Statistik Telekomunikasi Indonesia menyajikan data mengenai perkembangan sektor 
telekomunikasi di Indonesia, yang mencakup tingkat penetrasi internet, kepemilikan 
sarana teknologi informasi dan komunikasi (TIK), pola penggunaannya, serta data 
mengenai jaringan dan layanan telekomunikasi. Statistik ini memuat perkembangan 
internet di Indonesia, baik dari sisi penyedia layanan maupun pengguna. 
Menurut data BPS dari hasil pendataan Survei Sosial Ekonomi Nasional (Susenas) 2024, 
terdapat 72,78 persen penduduk Indonesia telah mengakses internet di tahun 2024 dan 
69,21 persen di tahun 2023. Tingginya penggunaan internet ini menunjukkan bahwa 
masyarakat memiliki akses yang lebih luas terhadap sumber informasi dan menjadi 
indikator kesiapan suatu negara atau wilayah dalam menghadapi era transformasi 
digital. 
Tingginya jumlah pengguna internet di Indonesia tidak terlepas dari pesatnya 
perkembangan telepon seluler. Pada tahun 2024 tercatat 68,65 persen penduduk di 
Indonesia telah memiliki telepon seluler. Angka ini mengalami  sedikit peningkatan jika 
dibandingkan dengan kondisi tahun 2023 yang mencapai 67,29 persen. 
Berbanding terbalik dengan perkembangan penggunaan telepon seluler yang terus 
meningkat dari tahun ke tahun, penggunaan telepon tetap kabel justru menunjukkan 
tren penurunan. Pada tahun 2024, tercatat hanya 0,99 persen rumah tangga yang 
memiliki/menguasai telepon tetap kabel, menurun jika dibandingkan dengan tahun 
2023 yang sebesar 1,17 persen. 
Kepemilikan komputer dalam rumah tangga tahun 2024 mengalami sedikit peningkatan 
jika dibandingkan dengan tahun 2023, yaitu sebesar 18,52 persen di tahun 2024 dan 
18,06 persen di tahun 2023. 
Pertumbuhan nilai ekspor barang TIK menunjukkan pola yang fluktuatif sepanjang 
tahun 2020 hingga 2024. Pada awal periode tersebut, ekspor barang TIK mencatatkan 
pertumbuhan positif, namun mengalami penurunan dalam dua tahun terakhir, yakni 
pada tahun 2023 dan 2024. Pada tahun 2024, nilai ekspor barang TIK Indonesia tercatat 
sebesar AS$7.371 juta, turun sebesar 2,22 persen dibandingkan tahun 2023 yang 
mencapai AS$7.538 juta. Tren penurunan selama dua tahun terakhir ini mengindikasikan 
RINGKASAN 
EKSEKUTIF
https://www.bps.go.id


===== PDF PAGE 28 =====
xxvi
melemahnya kinerja sektor TIK dalam mendukung ekspor nasional, serta menegaskan 
perlunya evaluasi terhadap daya saing dan ketahanan industri TIK di tengah dinamika 
ekonomi global yang terus berubah. 
Pertumbuhan impor barang TIK menunjukkan pola yang fluktuatif sepanjang periode 
2020–2024. Pada tahun 2024, nilai impor tercatat sebesar US$13.552 juta, meningkat 
sebesar 4,59 persen dibandingkan tahun sebelumnya. Di tahun 2024, sebagian besar 
impor barang TIK adalah kelompok komponen elektrik ( electric component ) sebesar 
US$4.939 juta dan kelompok komputer dan perangkat pendukung ( computers and 
peripheral equipment ) sebesar US$4.650 juta. Hal ini mengindikasikan beberapa 
kemungkinan, antara lain meningkatnya aktivitas perakitan atau produksi perangkat 
elektronik di dalam negeri. Selain itu, tingginya nilai impor komputer dan perangkat 
pendukung dapat mencerminkan meningkatnya kebutuhan akan infrastruktur digital, 
baik di sektor pemerintahan, pendidikan, maupun industri. 
Dalam struktur Produk Domestik Bruto (PDB), sektor informasi dan komunikasi 
menunjukkan ketahanan dan konsistensi pertumbuhan yang kuat selama lima tahun 
terakhir. Sektor ini memiliki pertumbuhan tertinggi keenam pada 2024 yaitu sebesar 7,57 
persen yang mencerminkan stabilnya permintaan terhadap layanan digital. Di sisi lain, 
meskipun memiliki peran strategis dalam mendukung transformasi digital dan inovasi, 
sektor informasi dan komunikasi belum memberikan kontribusi signifikan terhadap PDB. 
Dalam lima tahun terakhir, kontribusinya tercatat di bawah lima persen, dan pada tahun 
2024 hanya mencapai 4,34 persen, menempatkannya di posisi ketujuh dalam struktur 
ekonomi nasional. 
https://www.bps.go.id


===== PDF PAGE 29 =====
xxvii
Telecommunication Statistics Indonesia presents data on the development of the 
telecommunications sector in Indonesia, which includes internet penetration rates, 
ownership of information and communication technology (ICT) facilities, usage patterns, 
as well as data on telecommunications networks and services. This statistic contains the 
development of the internet in Indonesia, both in terms of service providers and users. 
According to BPS data from the results of the 2024 National Socio-Economic Survey 
(Susenas) data collection, 72.78 percent of the Indonesian population has accessed the 
internet in 2024 and 69.21 percent in 2023. This high internet usage shows that people have 
wider access to information sources and is an indicator of the readiness of a country or 
region in facing the era of digital transformation. 
The high number of internet users in Indonesia is inseparable from the rapid development 
of mobile cellular telephone. In 2024, it was recorded that 68.65 percent of the population in 
Indonesia had mobile cellular telephone. This figure has increased slightly when compared 
to the condition in 2023 which reached 67.29 percent.  
In contrast to the development of cellular telephone use which continues to increase from 
year to year, the use of fixed-line telephones actually shows a downward trend. In 2024, it 
was recorded that only 0.99 percent of households owned/operated a fixed-line telephone, 
a decrease compared to 2023 which amounted to 1.17 percent.  
Household computer ownership in 2024 experienced a slight increase compared to 2023, 
which amounted to 18.52 percent in 2024 and 18.06 percent in 2023.  
 The growth in the export value of ICT goods shows a fluctuating pattern from 2020 to 
2024. At the beginning of the period, exports of ICT goods recorded positive growth, but 
experienced a decline in the last two years, namely in 2023 and 2024. In 2024, the export 
value of Indonesia’s ICT goods was recorded at US$7,371 million, a decrease of 2.22 percent 
compared to 2023 which reached US$7,538 million. This downward trend over the past 
two years indicates the weakening performance of the ICT sector in supporting national 
exports, and emphasizes the need to evaluate the competitiveness and resilience of the ICT 
industry in the midst of changing global economic dynamics. 
EXECUTIVE 
SUMMARY
https://www.bps.go.id


===== PDF PAGE 30 =====
xxviii
The growth of imports of ICT goods shows a fluctuating pattern throughout the 2020–2024 
period. In 2024, the import value was recorded at US$13,552 million, an increase of 4.59 
percent compared to the previous year. Apart from 2024, only 2021 recorded positive 
growth, which amounted to 24.84 percent. In 2024, most of the imports of ICT goods were 
from the electrical components group (US$4,939 million) and the computers and peripheral 
equipment group (US$4,650 million). This indicates several possibilities, including increased 
assembly or production activities of electronic devices in the country. In addition, the high 
import value of computers and peripheral equipment may reflect the increasing need for 
digital infrastructure, both in the government, education and industrial sectors. 
Within the Gross Domestic Product (GDP) structure, the information and communication 
sector has shown strong resilience and consistent growth over the past five years. This 
sector has the sixth highest growth in 2024 at 7.57 percent, reflecting the stable demand 
for digital services. On the other hand, despite its strategic role in supporting digital 
transformation and innovation, the information and communication sector has yet to 
contribute significantly to GDP . In the last five years, its contribution has been recorded 
below five percent, and in 2024 it will only reach 4.34 percent, placing it in seventh place in 
the national economic structure.

1.1 GAMBARAN UMUM
Perkembangan TIK di Indonesia 
berkembang pesat. 
Perkembangan teknologi informasi 
dan komunikasi (TIK) di Indonesia telah 
mengalami transformasi signifikan dalam 
dua dekade terakhir, sejalan dengan 
meningkatnya kebutuhan masyarakat 
terhadap konektivitas, efisiensi, dan akses 
informasi secara real-time. Di era digital 
saat ini, TIK tidak lagi hanya menjadi 
pelengkap, melainkan telah berperan 
sebagai infrastruktur fundamental dalam 
mendukung pembangunan ekonomi, 
pemerintahan, pendidikan, hingga 
pelayanan publik.
Menurut Survei Sosial Ekonomi 
Nasional (Susenas) tahun 2024, persentase 
penduduk yang mengakses internet 
mencapai 72,78 persen, meningkat 
secara konsisten dibanding tahun-tahun 
sebelumnya, dimana pada 2021 hanya 
sebesar 62,10 persen. Pertumbuhan ini 
didorong oleh berbagai faktor, antara 
lain penetrasi smartphone  yang meluas, 
peningkatan daya beli masyarakat, serta 
ekspansi jaringan internet ke wilayah-
wilayah luar Jawa.
Dari sisi perangkat TIK, sebanyak 
92,92 persen rumah tangga di Indonesia 
1.1 OVERVIEW
The development of ICT in Indonesia is 
growing rapidly.
The development of information 
and communication technology (ICT) in 
Indonesia has undergone a significant 
transformation over the past two decades, 
in line with the increasing public demand 
for connectivity, efficiency, and real-time 
access to information. In today’s digital 
era, ICT is no longer merely a complement 
to daily life—it has become a fundamental 
infrastructure that supports economic 
development, governance, education, and 
public services.
According to the 2024 National 
Socio-Economic Survey (Susenas), the 
percentage of the population accessing the 
internet reached 72.78 percent, continuing 
a steady increase compared to previous 
years, where in 2021 the figure stood at 
only 62.10 percent. This growth has been 
driven by several factors, including the 
widespread penetration of smartphones, 
rising consumer purchasing power, and the 
expansion of internet networks to regions 
beyond Java.
In terms of ICT devices, 92.92 percent 
of Indonesian households owned a mobile

equitable access to ICT . These actions 
aligned with the “Digital Indonesia 2045”

Persentase rumah tangga yang memiliki

persen.

penting dalam mengukur penetrasi 
perangkat teknologi di tingkat rumah 
tangga. Berdasarkan data Survei Sosial

relatif rendah. Pada tahun 2024, tercatat

dibandingkan tahun-tahun sebelumnya.

di wilayah perdesaan. Persentase rumah 
tangga yang memiliki komputer di 
perdesaan meningkat dari 8,82 persen 
pada tahun 2021 menjadi 8,98 persen pada 
tahun 2024. Sebaliknya, wilayah perkotaan 
in achieving national digital sovereignty.
Nonetheless, challenges remain, 
particularly in addressing the uneven 
distribution of digital infrastructure between 
urban and rural areas and improving digital 
literacy among segments of the population. 
Digital transformation is no longer a choice 
but a strategic necessity for national 
progress. By optimizing the potential of ICT 
in an inclusive and sustainable manner, 
Indonesia is expected to thrive in the global 
digital era and foster a smart, productive, 
and competitive digital society.

percent of households were recorded as

previous years.

households owning a computer increased 
from 8.82 percent in 2021 to 8.98 percent in

a slight decline, from 25.40 percent in 2021

1.3 PERKEMBANGAN TELEPON 
KABEL TETAP
Persentase rumah tangga yang memiliki/
menguasai telepon tetap kabel sekitar 
0,99  persen. Artinya, dari 100 rumah 
tangga terdapat 1 rumah tangga yang 
memiliki telepon tetap kabel. 
Seiring dengan pesatnya 
perkembangan teknologi komunikasi, 
penggunaan telepon tetap kabel di 
lingkup rumah tangga terus mengalami 
penurunan. Pada tahun 2024, hanya 0,99 
persen rumah tangga yang masih memiliki 
atau menguasai telepon tetap kabel. 
Angka ini menurun jika dibandingkan 
dengan tahun 2021, yang mencapai 1,36 
persen. Penurunan ini mencerminkan 
pergeseran preferensi masyarakat menuju 
teknologi komunikasi yang lebih modern 
dan fleksibel, seperti telepon seluler dan 
layanan komunikasi berbasis internet.
1.3 THE DEVELOPMENT OF FIXED 
LINE TELEPHONE
The percentage of households owning 
fixed-line telephones was around 
0.99 percent. It means that out of 100 
households, 1 households had fixed-line 
phones.
Along with the rapid development 
of communication technology, the use of 
landline telephones at the household level 
has continued to decline. In 2024, only 
0.99 percent of households still owned or 
had access to a landline telephone. This 
figure has decreased compared to 2021, 
when it reached 1.36 percent. The decline 
reflects a shift in public preference toward 
more modern and flexible communication 
technologies, such as mobile phones and 
internet-based communication services.

due to some households in certain areas

infrastructure or limited access to mobile 
networks in remote locations.

1.4 THE DEVELOPMENT OF MOBILE 
CELLULAR TELEPHONE
In 2024, 68.65 percent of Indonesia’s 
population owned mobile cellular 
telephone.
Indonesia, with its high population 
spread among thousands of islands, needs 
a communication medium that can connect 
people with each other. In this context, 
the presence of mobile cellular telephone 
has an important and strategic role as 
the main tool in daily communication, 
allowing people to communicate without 
time and place restrictions. In addition, the 
wider penetration of the internet has also 
encouraged people to utilize mobile phones 
not only as a communication tool, but 
also as a multifunctional tool. People now 
easily access various other digital services 
and even their primary livelihood through 
various digital platforms. 
Sebaliknya, tingkat kepemilikan terendah 
tercatat di wilayah Maluku dan Papua, 
yakni sebesar 0,37 persen.

Pandemi COVID-19 memberikan 
dampak signifikan terhadap berbagai 
aspek kehidupan masyarakat, termasuk 
dalam hal kepemilikan telepon seluler. 
Sebelum pandemi terjadi, pada tahun 
2019, persentase penduduk Indonesia 
yang memiliki atau menguasai telepon 
seluler tercatat sebesar 63,53 persen. 
Namun, ketika pandemi mulai merebak 
pada tahun 2020, angka ini mengalami 
sedikit penurunan menjadi 62,84 
persen. Penurunan tersebut diduga 
disebabkan oleh berkurangnya atau 
bahkan hilangnya sumber penghasilan 
masyarakat selama masa krisis, sehingga 
memengaruhi kemampuan mereka dalam 
memenuhi kebutuhan sekunder, termasuk 
kepemilikan perangkat telekomunikasi.
Pada tahun 2021, meskipun pandemi 
COVID-19 masih menunjukkan peningkatan 
kasus dan mobilitas masyarakat di ruang 
publik tetap dibatasi, sejumlah aktivitas 
mulai disesuaikan agar dapat tetap 
berlangsung, termasuk pelaksanaan 
pembelajaran secara daring. Kondisi ini 
menyebabkan meningkatnya permintaan 
The development of mobile cellular 
telephone in Indonesia continues to rise, 
marked by the increase in mobile cellular 
telephone ownership. This is driven by 
several factors such as the size of the device 
which is getting smaller and lighter make it 
easy to carry. In 2010, there was only 38.05 
percent of the population owned/had full 
access to a cellular phone. Meanwhile, 
in 2024, 68.65 percent of the population 
owned a cellular phone. This means that 
over fifteen years, the number of people 
who own/control a cell phone has increased 
by 30.60 percent.
The Covid-19 pandemic has also 
influenced the development of mobile 
cellular telephone ownership. Before the 
pandemic occurred, 63.53 percent of the 
population owned mobile cellular telephone 
in 2019. This condition had decreased to 
62.84 percent when the pandemic began 
to spread in 2020. In order to prevent 
the spread of the virus, travel restriction 
policies, closing schools and offices, and 
recommendations to stay at home were 
implemented. It is indicated that the decline 
in mobile cellular telephone ownership is 
due to reduced or lost sources of income 
during the pandemic, so that residents have 
difficulty meeting their needs, one of which 
is a mobile cellular telephone.
In 2021, despite the COVID-19 
pandemic still showing an increase in cases 
and people’s mobility in public spaces 
remaining restricted, a number of activities 
have begun to be adapted to continue, 
including the implementation of online 
learning. This has led to increased demand 
for digital devices, especially cellular mobile

 The level of mobile cellular telephone 
ownership in urban areas is higher than in 
rural areas. In 2024, 74.15 percent of urban 
residents will own a cellular phone. In rural 
areas, only 60.78 percent of the population 
owns/controls a mobile cellular telephone. 
 
This condition reflects two things. 
First, urban residents have a higher 
demand for mobile cellular telephones 
because they need them to support the 
high level of activity in urban areas. Second, 
urban residents generally have higher 
incomes compared to rural residents, so 
they are more able to afford mobile cellular 
telephone devices. 
The level of mobile cellular telephone 
ownership in each province is relatively 
diverse. In general, the western provinces of 
Indonesia tend to have higher values than 
the eastern provinces.
In 2024, Kepulauan Riau Province 
recorded the highest percentage of mobile 
cellular telephone ownership in Indonesia, 
at 83.90 percent. In addition, the other 
four provinces that also occupy the highest 
position in terms of the percentage of 
people who own or control cellular phones 
are DKI Jakarta, East Kalimantan, North 
Kalimantan, and Central Kalimantan. The 
high rate of cellular phone ownership in 
these provinces indicates that access to 
communication technology in these areas 
is relatively better than in other provinces in 
Indonesia.

Meanwhile, conditions in the Maluku

1.5 PROGRESS OF INTERNET 
ACCESS
Internet access among the Indonesian 
population continues to increase.
Internet usage in Indonesia has 
shown an upward trend during the 2021–
2024 period. In 2021, around 62.10 percent 
of the population aged five and above 
reported having accessed the internet in 
the past three months. This figure rose to 
72.78 percent in 2024. The increase occurred 
across all regions, both urban and rural. In 
urban areas, the percentage of internet 
users rose from 71.81 percent in 2021 to 
79.14 percent in 2024. Meanwhile, in rural 
areas, the increase was more significant, 
from 49.30 percent to 63.71 percent over the 
same period. This data reflects the growing 
reach and adoption of digital technology 
across various segments of society.
Sementara itu, kondisi di kelompok 
Pulau Maluku dan Papua menunjukkan 
tantangan yang lebih besar. Hanya 32,24 
persen desa/kelurahan di kawasan ini 
yang menerima sinyal kuat, dan 10,63 
persen menerima sinyal sangat kuat. 
Sisanya, 32,05 persen masih berada dalam 
cakupan sinyal lemah, dan proporsi desa/
kelurahan yang belum terjangkau sinyal 
mencapai angka yang cukup tinggi, yaitu 
25,07 persen. 
Perbedaan ini mencerminkan 
ketimpangan dalam akses infrastruktur 
telekomunikasi antar wilayah di Indonesia, 
serta menunjukkan perlunya perhatian 
khusus terhadap pengembangan jaringan 
telekomunikasi di kawasan timur 
Indonesia.
1.5 PERKEMBANGAN AKSES 
INTERNET
Akses internet di kalangan penduduk 
Indonesia terus meningkat.
Penggunaan internet di Indonesia 
menunjukkan tren peningkatan dalam 
periode 2021–2024. Pada tahun 2021, 
sekitar 62,10 persen penduduk berusia lima 
tahun ke atas tercatat pernah mengakses 
internet dalam tiga bulan terakhir. Angka 
ini meningkat menjadi 72,78 persen 
pada tahun 2024. Peningkatan ini terjadi 
di seluruh wilayah, baik di perkotaan 
maupun perdesaan. Di daerah perkotaan, 
persentase pengguna internet naik dari 
71,81 persen pada 2021 menjadi 79,14 
persen pada 2024. Sementara itu, di daerah 
perdesaan, persentasenya meningkat lebih 
tajam, dari 49,30 persen menjadi 63,71 
persen pada periode yang sama. Data ini 
mencerminkan semakin meluasnya akses 
dan adopsi teknologi digital di berbagai 
lapisan masyarakat.

also served as popular access points, used 
by around 51.62 percent of the population.

environments dominate internet usage, 
public spaces continue to play a significant 
role in supporting digital connectivity within 
society.

PERANAN TIK DALAM PEREKONOMIAN 
The Role of ICT in the Economy
2.1 PERKEMBANGAN EKSPOR 
BARANG TIK
Ekspor barang-barang TIK Indonesia 
mencapai US$ 7.371 juta di tahun 2024. 
Ekspor merupakan salah satu pilar 
utama dalam struktur perekonomian 
Indonesia, yang berperan penting dalam 
mendukung pertumbuhan nasional, 
menciptakan lapangan kerja, dan 
meningkatkan devisa negara. Di antara 
berbagai sektor yang berkembang, ekspor 
produk teknologi informasi dan komunikasi 
(TIK) menjadi salah satu indikator penting 
kemajuan industri digital nasional. Kinerja 
ekspor TIK mencerminkan tidak hanya 
kemampuan inovasi dan manufaktur 
dalam negeri, tetapi juga kesiapan 
Indonesia menghadapi transformasi 
ekonomi berbasis teknologi.
Pertumbuhan nilai ekspor barang 
TIK menunjukkan pola yang fluktuatif 
sepanjang tahun 2020 hingga 2024. Pada 
awal periode tersebut, ekspor barang TIK 
mencatatkan pertumbuhan positif, namun 
mengalami penurunan dalam dua tahun 
terakhir, yakni pada tahun 2023 dan 2024. 
Pada tahun 2024, nilai ekspor barang TIK 
Indonesia tercatat sebesar AS$7.371 juta, 
turun sebesar 2,22  persen dibandingkan 
tahun 2023 yang mencapai AS$7.538 juta. 
2.1 THE PERFORMANCE OF ICT 
GOODS EXPORTS
Indonesia’s exports of ICT goods reached 
US$ 7,371 million in 2024.
Exports constitute one of the main 
pillars of Indonesia’s economic structure, 
playing a vital role in supporting national 
growth, creating employment opportunities, 
and increasing foreign exchange earnings. 
Among the various developing sectors, the 
export of information and communication 
technology (ICT) products serves as a key 
indicator of the progress of the national 
digital industry. The performance of 
ICT exports reflects not only domestic 
innovation and manufacturing capabilities 
but also Indonesia’s readiness to 
embrace a technology-driven economic 
transformation.
The growth of ICT goods export value 
exhibited a fluctuating pattern throughout 
2020 to 2024. At the beginning of this period, 
ICT exports recorded positive growth; 
however, they experienced a decline over 
the last two years, namely in 2023 and 2024. 
In 2024, Indonesia’s ICT export value was 
recorded at US$7,371 million, representing 
a decrease of 2.22 percent compared to 
US$7,538 million in 2023. Meanwhile, the 
export value in 2023 also declined by 1.49 
BAB/Chapter 2

Statistik Telekomunikasi Indonesia 2024/Telecommunication Statistics in Indonesia 2024
28
Sementara itu, nilai ekspor tahun 2023 
juga mengalami penurunan sebesar 1,49 
persen dibandingkan tahun sebelumnya. 
Tren penurunan selama dua tahun 
terakhir ini mengindikasikan melemahnya 
kinerja sektor TIK dalam mendukung 
ekspor nasional, serta menegaskan 
perlunya evaluasi terhadap daya saing dan 
ketahanan industri TIK di tengah dinamika 
ekonomi global yang terus berubah.
Kontribusi ekspor barang TIK 
terhadap total ekspor Indonesia 
menunjukkan tren penurunan, dengan 
persentase yang konsisten berada di 
bawah empat persen. Dari 3,33 persen pada 
tahun 2020, kontribusi tersebut menurun 
menjadi 2,78 persen pada tahun 2024. 
Kondisi ini mengindikasikan bahwa produk 
TIK belum menjadi komoditas utama 
dalam struktur ekspor nasional, sehingga 
diperlukan strategi penguatan agar sektor 
ini dapat memberikan kontribusi yang 
lebih signifikan dalam mendukung kinerja 
perdagangan luar negeri Indonesia.
Secara lebih rinci, kelompok barang 
TIK dengan nilai ekspor terbesar pada 
tahun 2020 serta periode 2022 hingga 
2024 adalah peralatan komunikasi 
(communication equipment ), yang 
mencakup produk seperti smartphone, 
modem, dan telepon. Sementara itu, pada 
tahun 2021, kelompok barang TIK dengan 
nilai ekspor tertinggi bergeser ke peralatan 
elektronik rumah tangga atau pribadi 
(consumer electric equipment ), seperti 
televisi, kulkas, dan kamera digital.
Terjadinya pergeseran kategori 
barang TIK dengan nilai ekspor 
terbesar dari peralatan komunikasi ke 
peralatan elektronik konsumen pada 
tahun 2021 menunjukkan adanya 
percent compared to the previous year. This 
downward trend over the past two years 
indicates a weakening performance of the 
ICT sector in supporting national exports 
and underscores the need for evaluating 
the competitiveness and resilience of the 
ICT industry amid the continuously evolving 
global economic dynamics.
In terms of contribution to Indonesia’s 
total exports, the export of ICT goods has 
also shown a declining trend, with its 
percentage consistently remaining below 
four percent. In 2020, the contribution of ICT 
goods exports was recorded at 3.33 percent 
and continued to decline, reaching 2.78 
percent in 2024. This condition indicates 
that ICT products have yet to become one 
of the main commodities in the national 
export structure, highlighting the need 
for strengthening strategies so that this 
sector can play a more significant role 
in supporting Indonesia’s foreign trade 
performance.
More specifically, the ICT product 
group with the highest export value in 2020 
as well as during the period from 2022 to 
2024 was communication equipment, which 
includes products such as smartphones, 
modems, and telephones. Meanwhile, 
in 2021, the ICT product group with the 
highest export value shifted to consumer 
electric equipment, such as televisions, 
refrigerators, and digital cameras.
The shift in the ICT product category 
with the highest export value from 
communication equipment to consumer 
electronic equipment in 2021 reflects the 
dynamics of global market demand as

Indonesia’s imports of ICT goods reached 
US$ 13,552 million in 2024.
The import of information and 
communication technology (ICT) goods 
reflects domestic demand for digitalization-
enabling devices. As digital transformation 
accelerates across various sectors, trends 
in ICT imports serve as a critical indicator 
of Indonesia’s reliance on foreign products

30
ketergantungan Indonesia terhadap 
produk luar negeri serta kesiapan industri 
nasional dalam memenuhi permintaan 
pasar.
Kontribusi impor barang TIK dalam 
struktur impor nasional menunjukkan 
tren penurunan selama lima tahun 
terakhir, dari 9,36 persen pada tahun 2020 
menjadi 5,80 persen pada tahun 2024. 
Penurunan ini dapat mengindikasikan 
beberapa kemungkinan, antara lain: 
meningkatnya kapasitas produksi barang 
TIK dalam negeri, pergeseran konsumsi 
dari perangkat keras ke perangkat lunak 
atau layanan teknologi informasi dan 
komunikasi yang tidak tercatat sebagai 
impor barang, serta diversifikasi struktur 
impor seiring meningkatnya permintaan 
pada sektor-sektor lainnya.
Selain kontribusi terhadap total 
impor, pola pertumbuhan tahunan 
juga menunjukkan dinamika yang 
menarik. Pertumbuhan impor barang 
TIK menunjukkan pola yang fluktuatif 
sepanjang periode 2020–2024. Pada 
tahun 2024, nilai impor tercatat sebesar 
US$13.552 juta, meningkat sebesar 4,59   
persen dibandingkan tahun sebelumnya. 
Selain tahun 2024, hanya tahun 2021 
yang mencatat pertumbuhan positif, yaitu 
sebesar 24,84 persen, sementara pada 
tahun 2020, 2022, dan 2023, pertumbuhan 
tercatat negatif.
Pertumbuhan yang positif dapat 
diartikan dari dua sisi. Dari sisi positif, 
peningkatan impor TIK bisa mencerminkan 
permintaan tinggi terhadap perangkat 
dan teknologi, indikasi aktivitas ekonomi 
yang sehat, serta transfer teknologi dan 
peningkatan produktivitas. Dari sisi 
negatif, kenaikan impor menunjukkan 
ketergantungan pada produk luar negeri.
and the readiness of its domestic industry to 
meet market demand.
The contribution of ICT goods to the 
national import structure has shown a 
declining trend over the past five years, 
falling from 9.36 percent in 2020 to 5.80 
percent in 2024. This decline may suggest 
several possibilities: an increase in domestic 
production capacity of ICT goods, a shift in 
consumption from hardware to software 
or ICT services—which are not recorded as 
goods imports—as well as diversification in 
the import structure due to rising demand 
in other sectors.
In addition to their share of total 
imports, the annual growth pattern of ICT 
imports also reveals important dynamics. 
The value of ICT imports has fluctuated 
throughout the 2020–2024 period. In 2024, 
the total value of imports reached US$13,552 
million, an increase of 4.59 percent 
compared to the previous year. Apart from 
2024, only 2021 recorded positive growth at 
24.84 percent, while in 2020, 2022, and 2023, 
growth was negative.
Positive import growth can be 
interpreted from two perspectives. On the 
positive side, it may reflect strong demand 
for devices and technology, indicating 
healthy economic activity, technology 
transfer, and improved productivity. On the 
other hand, rising imports may also signal a 
continued dependence on foreign products.

31
Peranan TIK Dalam Perekonomian/The Role of ICT in  the Economy
In 2024, the majority of ICT imports 
consisted of electric components, valued 
at US$4,939 million, and computers and 
peripheral equipment, valued at US$4,650 
million. Electric components typically serve 
as parts for electronic devices or are used 
in electronic manufacturing, including 
items such as semiconductors, capacitors, 
and resistors. Meanwhile, computers and 
peripheral equipment encompass primary 
computing devices and their supporting 
accessories, such as desktop and laptop 
computers, servers, keyboards, mice, and 
similar items.
This pattern is consistent with the 
years 2022–2023. In contrast, during 2020–
2021, the composition of ICT imports showed 
a different trend, with communication 
equipment being the category with the 
highest import value. This shift indicates a 
change in Indonesia’s ICT import structure—
from the dominance of communication 
equipment in 2020–2021 to a focus on 
electric components and computing devices 
during 2022–2024.
This trend may imply several 
developments, including increased 
domestic activity in the assembly or 
production of electronic devices. In addition, 
the high import value of computers and 
related equipment may reflect growing 
demand for digital infrastructure across 
government, education, and industrial 
sectors. Meanwhile, the decline in 
communication device imports may be 
attributed to substitution by domestically 
produced goods, particularly items such as 
smartphones and modems.
Di tahun 2024, sebagian besar impor 
barang TIK adalah kelompok komponen 
elektrik ( electric component ) sebesar 
US$4.939 juta dan kelompok komputer 
dan perangkat pendukung ( computers 
and peripheral equipment ) sebesar 
US$4.650 juta. Komponen elektrik adalah 
komponen yang biasanya digunakan 
sebagai bagian dari perangkat elektronik 
atau untuk keperluan produksi di sektor 
elektronik, seperti semikonduktor, 
kapasitor, dan resistor. Sementara itu, 
komputer dan perangkat pendukung 
mencakup perangkat utama dan aksesoris 
pendukung sistem komputer, seperti 
komputer desktop dan laptop, server, 
keyboard dan mouse, dan sebagainya.
Pola ini konsisten dengan tahun 2022–
2023. Sebaliknya, pada tahun 2020–2021, 
komposisi impor barang TIK menunjukkan 
pola yang berbeda, dengan perangkat 
komunikasi ( communication equipment ) 
sebagai kelompok dengan nilai impor 
tertinggi. Perubahan ini mencerminkan 
adanya pergeseran struktur impor barang 
TIK Indonesia, dari dominasi perangkat 
komunikasi pada 2020–2021 ke dominasi 
komponen elektrik serta komputer dan 
perangkat pendukung pada 2022–2024.
Hal ini mengindikasikan beberapa 
kemungkinan, antara lain meningkatnya 
aktivitas perakitan atau produksi 
perangkat elektronik di dalam negeri. 
Selain itu, tingginya nilai impor komputer 
dan perangkat pendukung dapat 
mencerminkan meningkatnya kebutuhan 
akan infrastruktur digital, baik di sektor 
pemerintahan, pendidikan, maupun 
industri. Sementara itu, penurunan impor 
perangkat komunikasi kemungkinan 
disebabkan oleh substitusi dengan produk 
lokal, khususnya untuk barang seperti 
smartphone dan modem.

2.3 ICT SECTOR IN GDP
In 2024, the information and 
communication sector grew by 7.57 
percent compared to the previous year.
The information and communication 
technology (ICT) sector plays a vital role 
in driving Indonesia’s economic growth 
by enhancing productivity, efficiency, and 
innovation across various industries. The 
development of this sector also reflects the 
progress of digital transformation, which 
has become one of the main pillars of 
national economic development.
Within the structure of the Gross 
Domestic Product (GDP), the information 
and communication sector comprises: (1) 
publishing activities; (2) motion picture, video 
and television program production, sound 
recording, and music publishing activities; 
(3) broadcasting and programming

2.4 HOUSEHOLD ICT EXPENDITURE
In 2024, household expenditure on 
telecommunications amounted to Rp 
212 thousand, or 3.52 percent of total 
household spending.
Household expenditure on Information 
and Communication Technology (ICT) 
is a vital aspect in understanding the 
consumption patterns of modern society. 
Such expenditure reflects the extent to which 
digital technology has become an integral 
part of daily life, while also providing 
insight into household-level access to and 
utilization of telecommunication services 
and digital technologies. ICT expenditure 
in this context includes payments for 
telephone services, mobile phone credit, 
internet fees, internet café usage, mobile 
phone purchases, and related items.
During the 2021 to 2024 period, 
the percentage of households with ICT 
expenditure consistently remained above 90 
percent. In 2024, 93.16 percent of households 
were recorded to have ICT -related spending. 
There is a notable disparity based on area 
classification, with 95.31 percent of urban 
households reporting ICT expenditure 
compared to 90.10 percent in rural areas.
This indicates that nearly all 
households in Indonesia allocate a portion 
of their spending to ICT needs, underscoring 
the essential role of technology in everyday 
life. However, the urban-rural gap suggests 
ongoing disparities in access to and use of 
ICT . Furthermore, the small percentage of 
households that do not incur ICT -related 
expenses highlights segments of the 
population that remain unserved or have yet 
2.4 PENGELUARAN TIK RUMAH 
TANGGA
Pada tahun 2024, pengeluaran rumah 
tangga untuk telekomunikasi sebesar 
Rp 212 ribu atau 3,52  persen dari total

Pengeluaran rumah tangga untuk 
Teknologi Informasi dan Komunikasi 
(TIK) merupakan aspek penting dalam 
memahami pola konsumsi masyarakat 
modern. Pengeluaran tersebut 
mencerminkan sejauh mana teknologi 
digital telah menjadi bagian integral 
dalam kehidupan sehari-hari, sekaligus 
memberikan gambaran mengenai akses 
dan pemanfaatan layanan telekomunikasi 
serta teknologi digital di tingkat rumah 
tangga. Pengeluaran TIK dalam hal ini 
mencakup pembayaran telepon, pulsa 
telepon seluler, biaya internet, warnet, 
pembelian HP , dan sebagainya.
Selama periode 2021 hingga 2024, 
persentase rumah tangga yang memiliki 
pengeluaran untuk TIK konsisten berada 
di atas 90 persen. Pada tahun 2024, 
sebanyak 93,16 persen rumah tangga 
tercatat memiliki pengeluaran untuk TIK. 
Terdapat perbedaan yang cukup signifikan 
berdasarkan klasifikasi daerah, di mana 
persentase rumah tangga di perkotaan 
mencapai 95,31 persen, sedangkan di 
perdesaan sebesar 90,10 persen.
Hal tersebut menunjukkan bahwa 
hampir seluruh rumah tangga di Indonesia 
mengalokasikan sebagian pengeluarannya 
untuk kebutuhan TIK, yang mencerminkan 
peran penting teknologi dalam kehidupan 
sehari-hari. Namun, perbedaan antara 
daerah perkotaan dan perdesaan 
mengindikasikan adanya kesenjangan 
akses dan penggunaan TIK. Selain itu,

kecil, menunjukkan adanya kelompok

dan komunikasi secara penuh. Hal ini

Therefore, these findings underscore the 
need to prioritize efforts toward equitable 
digitalization and technological inclusion 
across all regions of Indonesia.

Rata-rata pengeluaran rumah tangga

menjadi komponen tetap dan signifikan 
dalam struktur pengeluaran rumah 
In  2024, the average monthly household 
expenditure on telecommunications 
amounted to IDR 212,000 or 3.52 percent of 
total household spending. This indicates that 
telecommunications have become a fixed 
and significant component of household 
expenditure in Indonesia. The stable share

38
tangga di Indonesia. Stabilnya persentase 
pengeluaran di kisaran 3,5 persen selama 
periode 2021–2024 mencerminkan bahwa 
kebutuhan akan akses komunikasi dan 
konektivitas digital tetap konsisten, 
bahkan di tengah dinamika ekonomi dan 
perubahan pola konsumsi.
Hal ini juga dapat diartikan bahwa 
rumah tangga telah menganggap layanan 
telekomunikasi sebagai kebutuhan 
pokok, sejajar dengan kebutuhan dasar 
lainnya seperti makanan, pendidikan, 
dan transportasi. Di sisi lain, stabilitas ini 
mengindikasikan adanya efisiensi atau 
kestabilan harga layanan TIK yang tidak 
membebani pengeluaran rumah tangga 
secara signifikan, meskipun penggunaan 
teknologi terus meningkat.
Sementara itu, rata-rata pengeluaran 
di daerah perkotaan lebih tinggi 
dibandingkan perdesaan, yaitu Rp 252 
ribu di daerah perkotaan, sedangkan di 
daerah perdesaan Rp 151 ribu. Perbedaan 
ini mencerminkan adanya kesenjangan 
dalam akses, intensitas penggunaan, dan 
kualitas layanan TIK antarwilayah. Rumah 
tangga di perkotaan cenderung memiliki 
kebutuhan yang lebih tinggi terhadap 
layanan digital, baik untuk pekerjaan, 
pendidikan, hiburan, maupun aktivitas 
ekonomi yang semakin berbasis daring. 
Sebaliknya, lebih rendahnya pengeluaran 
di perdesaan dapat mengindikasikan 
terbatasnya infrastruktur TIK, rendahnya 
tingkat adopsi teknologi, atau kemampuan 
ekonomi yang masih terbatas. Kondisi 
ini menegaskan pentingnya upaya 
pemerataan akses dan peningkatan 
literasi digital di seluruh wilayah Indonesia 
agar manfaat transformasi digital dapat 
dirasakan secara lebih inklusif dan merata.
Komposisi pengeluaran rumah 
tangga di Indonesia pada tahun 2021–2024 
of telecommunications spending, around 
3.5 percent throughout 2021–2024, reflects 
consistent demand for communication 
access and digital connectivity, even amid 
economic fluctuations and changing 
consumption behaviors.
This also suggests that households 
now regard telecommunications as a basic 
necessity, on par with food, education, and 
transportation. On the other hand, the 
stability in expenditure may indicate either 
efficiency or relatively steady pricing of ICT 
services, which prevents significant strain 
on household budgets despite the growing 
adoption of technology.
Meanwhile, average spending in 
urban areas is higher than in rural ones—
IDR 252,000 per month in urban households 
compared to IDR 151,000 in rural areas. 
This difference reflects disparities in access, 
usage intensity, and service quality between 
regions. Urban households tend to have 
greater needs for digital services for work, 
education, entertainment, and increasingly 
online-based economic activities. 
Conversely, lower spending in rural areas 
may indicate limited ICT infrastructure, 
lower adoption rates, or financial 
limitations. This condition reinforces the 
importance of promoting equal access and 
enhancing digital literacy nationwide so 
that the benefits of digital transformation 
can be shared more inclusively and 
equitably across Indonesia.

PENYELENGGARA TELEKOMUNIKASI 
Telecommunication Providers
BAB/Chapter 3
3.1 PENYELENGGARA 
TELEKOMUNIKASI
Penyelenggara telekomunikasi di 
Indonesia sebanyak 2.272 di tahun 2024. 
Penyelenggara telekomunikasi 
memegang peran strategis dalam 
mendukung infrastruktur komunikasi 
modern yang menjadi fondasi berbagai 
sektor kehidupan, mulai dari ekonomi, 
pendidikan, hingga pemerintahan. Dalam 
skala global, industri telekomunikasi 
mengalami perkembangan pesat 
seiring kemajuan teknologi digital dan 
meningkatnya kebutuhan masyarakat 
terhadap konektivitas yang cepat, andal, 
dan luas.
Di Indonesia, hingga tahun 2024, 
tercatat sebanyak 2.272 penyelenggara 
telekomunikasi yang terdiri atas 639 
penyelenggara jaringan tetap, 22 
penyelenggara jaringan bergerak, 1.435 
penyelenggara jaringan bergerak satelit, 
dan 176 penyelenggara telekomunikasi 
khusus. Seluruh penyelenggara tersebut 
berperan penting dalam mendorong 
pertumbuhan ekonomi digital serta 
mendukung transformasi sosial secara 
berkelanjutan.
3.1 TELECOMMUNICATION 
PROVIDER
In 2024, there were 2,272 
telecommunication providers in 
Indonesia.
Telecommunication providers play 
a strategic role in supporting modern 
communication infrastructure, which 
serves as the foundation for various 
sectors of life, including the economy, 
education, and government. Globally, 
the telecommunications industry has 
experienced rapid growth in line with 
advancements in digital technology and the 
increasing public demand for fast, reliable, 
and extensive connectivity.
In Indonesia, as of 2024, there were 
2,272 registered telecommunication 
providers, comprising 639 fixed network 
providers, 22 mobile network providers, 
1,435 mobile satellite network providers, and 
176 private telecommunication providers. 
These providers play a vital role in driving 
digital economic growth and supporting 
sustainable social transformation.

khusus menunjukkan tren peningkatan 
dari tahun ke tahun. Sebaliknya, jumlah

cenderung stabil dalam kurun waktu yang 
sama.
Perbedaan ini mencerminkan 
karakteristik dan tingkat kesulitan yang 
berbeda di setiap segmen industri. Jumlah 
penyelenggara jaringan tetap dan jasa 
telekomunikasi terus meningkat, karena 
kebutuhan akan konektivitas digital 
semakin besar dan regulasi bagi pelaku 
baru relatif lebih mudah, termasuk melalui 
skema berbagi jaringan. Sementara itu, 
jumlah penyelenggara jaringan bergerak 
tetap stabil karena membutuhkan 
investasi besar, spektrum frekuensi yang 
terbatas, serta pasar yang lebih tertutup, 
sehingga hanya sedikit pelaku baru yang 
dapat masuk.
Jumlah penyelenggara Internet 
Service Provider (ISP) mencapai 1.248 
perusahaan, dengan jumlah pelanggan 
17,05 juta di tahun 2024.
Salah satu penyedia layanan internet 
di Indonesia adalah Penyelenggara Jasa 
Internet (Internet Service Provider/ISP). ISP 
memiliki peran penting dalam memperluas 
akses internet yang cepat, stabil, dan 
andal bagi masyarakat serta pelaku usaha. 
Dibandingkan dengan layanan berbasis 
seluler, layanan ISP umumnya lebih unggul 
dalam hal kecepatan dan kestabilan, 
sehingga lebih sesuai untuk penggunaan 
tetap di rumah, kantor, maupun institusi.
Dalam lima tahun terakhir, jumlah 
ISP di Indonesia menunjukkan tren yang 
meningkat. Pada tahun 2020, tercatat 
sebanyak 473 ISP dan jumlah ini tumbuh 
signifikan hingga mencapai 1.248 ISP 
pada tahun 2024. Peningkatan ini 
mencerminkan tingginya permintaan akan 
of mobile network providers has remained 
relatively constant during the same period.
This disparity reflects the distinct 
characteristics and entry barriers within 
each segment of the industry. The steady 
growth in the number of fixed network 
and service providers is driven by the 
growing demand for digital connectivity 
and relatively accessible regulations for 
new entrants, including through network-
sharing schemes. Meanwhile, the number 
of mobile network providers remains 
stable due to the high investment required, 
limited frequency spectrum, and a more 
concentrated market structure, which 
makes it difficult for new players to enter 
the segment.
The number of Internet Service Provider 
(ISP) reached 1,248 companies, with a 
total of 17.05 million subscribers in 2024.
In Indonesia, one of the key providers 
of internet services is the Internet Service 
Provider (ISP). ISPs play an important role 
in expanding access to fast, stable, and 
reliable internet for both the public and 
businesses. Compared to mobile-based 
services, ISP services are generally superior 
in terms of speed and stability, making them 
more suitable for fixed usage in homes, 
offices, and institutions.
Over the past five years, the number 
of Internet Service Providers (ISPs) in 
Indonesia has shown an upward trend. In 
2020, there were 473 ISPs recorded, and 
this number grew significantly to reach 
1,248 ISPs by 2024. This increase reflects 
the high demand for fixed internet services

new providers, in line with the acceleration 
of digital transformation across various

more competitive service offerings from

supporting equitable digital access have 
also contributed to the growth of the 
subscriber base.

3.2 PELANGGAN TELEPON
Di tahun 2024, jumlah pelanggan telepon 
mencapai 351,97 juta, yang terdiri dari 
4,65 juta pelanggan telepon tetap dan 
347,32 juta pelanggan telepon seluler.
Pelanggan telepon merupakan 
individu atau entitas yang menggunakan 
layanan telekomunikasi untuk 
berkomunikasi jarak jauh. Seiring 
perkembangan zaman, layanan telepon 
telah mengalami perubahan dari sistem 
analog menjadi digital, mencakup telepon 
tetap hingga telepon seluler. Keberadaan 
pelanggan telepon mencerminkan tingkat 
kebutuhan masyarakat terhadap sarana 
komunikasi yang cepat dan efisien.
3.2 TELEPHONE SUBSCRIBERS
In 2024, the total number of telephone 
subscribers reached 351.97 million, 
consisting of 4.65 million fixed-line 
subscribers and 347.32 million mobile 
subscribers.
Telephone subscribers are individuals 
or entities that utilize telecommunication 
services to communicate over long 
distances. Over time, telephone services 
have evolved from analog systems to digital 
technologies, encompassing both fixed-
line and mobile phones. The presence of 
telephone subscribers reflects the level 
of public demand for fast and efficient 
communication tools.
https://www.bps.go.id


===== PDF PAGE 78 =====
Statistik Telekomunikasi Indonesia 2024/Telecommunication Statistics in Indonesia 2024
48
Dalam lima tahun terakhir, jumlah 
pelanggan telepon tetap terus mengalami 
penurunan. Penurunan paling tajam 
terjadi antara tahun 2023 dan 2024, 
yaitu sebesar 49,19 persen, dari 9,16 juta 
menjadi 4,65 juta pelanggan. Kondisi ini 
menunjukkan bahwa masyarakat semakin 
meninggalkan layanan telepon rumah 
dan beralih ke layanan komunikasi yang 
lebih fleksibel seperti telepon seluler atau 
aplikasi berbasis internet. Hal ini menjadi 
tanda bahwa keberadaan telepon tetap 
mulai tergeser oleh kemajuan teknologi 
digital.
Over the past five years, the number 
of fixed-line subscribers has continued to 
decline. The most significant drop occurred 
between 2023 and 2024, with a decrease 
of 49.19 percent, from 9.16 million to 4.65 
million subscribers. This trend indicates 
a shift in public preference away from 
landline services toward more flexible 
communication options such as mobile 
phones or internet-based applications. It 
also suggests that fixed-line telephony is 
gradually being displaced by advancements 
in digital technology.

Catatan Teknis/Technical Notes
CATATAN TEKNIS
Technical Notes
A. LATAR BELAKANG
Era masyarakat digital di Indonesia 
salah satunya ditunjukkan oleh pesatnya 
perkembangan Teknologi Informasi dan 
Komunikasi (TIK), khususnya industri 
telekomunikasi selama beberapa tahun 
terakhir ini. Telah terjadi pergeseran dari 
penggunaan telepon kabel tetap menjadi 
penggunaan telepon seluler, utamanya 
penggunaan internet melalui telepon 
seluler yang berkembang dengan pesat. 
Selain itu, karena semakin meningkatnya 
perkembangan telekomunikasi di 
Indonesia, saat ini dikenal istilah ”Ekonomi 
Digital” yang merambah di daerah 
perkotaan dan perdesaan di Indonesia. 
Telekomunikasi telah menghilangkan 
batas-batas jarak dan mereduksi 
perbedaan antara masyarakat di daerah 
perkotaan dengan perdesaan. Perbedaan 
waktu, jauhnya lokasi, serta heterogenitas 
karakteristik penduduk tidak lagi menjadi 
hambatan dalam kecepatan penyebaran 
informasi. 
Publikasi ini menyediakan data 
tentang informasi perkembangan 
telekomunikasi di Indonesia yang 
mencakup data jaringan telekomunikasi 
dan jasa telekomunikasi. Data jaringan 
telekomunikasi meliputi informasi 
statistik tentang operator penyelenggara 
telekomunikasi di Indonesia, 
A. INTRODUCTION
The era of digital society in Indonesia 
is shown by the rapid development 
of Information and Communication 
Technology (ICT), especially the 
telecommunications industry over the past 
few years. There has been a shift from the 
use of fixed-line telephones to the use of 
cellular telephones, especially the use of 
the internet through cellular telephones 
which is growing rapidly. In addition, 
due to the increasing development of 
telecommunications in Indonesia, there 
is now a term “Digital Economy” that 
has penetrated urban and rural areas 
in Indonesia. Telecommunications has 
eliminated the boundaries of distance and 
reduced the differences between people 
in urban and rural areas. Time difference, 
distance, and heterogeneity of population 
characteristics are no longer obstacles in 
the speed of information dissemination. 
This publication provides data on 
the development of telecommunications 
in Indonesia, which includes data 
on telecommunications networks 
and telecommunications services. 
Telecommunications network data 
includes statistical information on 
telecommunications operators in 
https://www.bps.go.id


===== PDF PAGE 88 =====
Statistik Telekomunikasi Indonesia 2024/Telecommunication Statistics in Indonesia 2024
58
telekomunikasi dengan kabel berupa 
telepon kabel tetap dan telekomunikasi 
tanpa kabel berupa telepon bergerak 
seluler, serta perkembangan internet di 
Indonesia, baik dari sisi penyelenggara 
maupun pengguna.
B. METODOLOGI
Sumber data dalam publikasi ini 
diperoleh dari hasil kegiatan BPS yaitu 
Survei Sosial Ekonomi Nasional (Susenas) 
dan Pendataan Potensi Desa (Podes) 
serta dari data sekunder instansi terkait 
seperti Kementerian Komunikasi dan 
Digital (Kemenkomdigi) dan perusahaan 
penyelenggara telekomunikasi.
Pengumpulan dan pengolahan 
data dilakukan oleh Direktorat Statistik 
Keuangan, Teknologi Informasi, dan 
Pariwisata, Badan Pusat Statistik. Penyajian 
data dilakukan dalam bentuk tabulasi atau 
grafik untuk menggambarkan frekuensi, 
perbandingan, proporsi, maupun 
perkembangan suatu indikator tertentu 
dengan metodologi sebagai berikut :
a. Mengumpulkan data sekunder 
dari instansi terkait seperti 
Kementerian Komunikasi dan Digital 
(Kemenkomdigi), serta laporan 
tahunan perusahaan penyelenggara 
telekomunikasi seperti PT 
Telekomunikasi  Indonesia   (Telkom), 
PT Telkomsel, PT Indosat, dan lain-lain.
b. Melakukan pengolahan data hasil 
survei yang pernah dilakukan BPS 
di antaranya Survei Sosial Ekonomi 
Nasional (Susenas) tahun 2019-2024, 
serta Pendataan Potensi Desa (Podes) 
tahun 2024.
c. Mempelajari jurnal dan makalah 
nasional maupun internasional 
Indonesia, telecommunications with lines 
in the form of fixed line telephones and 
telecommunications without lines in the 
form of mobile phones, as well as internet 
developments in Indonesia, both in terms of 
providers and users.
B. METHODOLOGY
Data sources in this publication are 
obtained from the results of BPS activities, 
namely the National Socio-Economic 
Survey (Susenas) and Village Potential Data 
Collection (Podes) as well as from secondary 
data from related institutions such as the 
Ministry of Communication and Digital 
(Kemenkomdigi) and telecommunications 
providers.
Data collection and processing 
is carried out by the Directorate of 
Financial, Information Technology, and 
Tourism Statistics, Statistics Indonesia. 
Data presentation is done in the form of 
tabulations or graphs to illustrate the 
frequency, comparison, proportion, or 
development of a particular indicator with 
the following methodology:
a. Collect secondary data from relevant 
agencies such as the Ministry 
of Communication and Digital 
(Kemenkomdigi), as well as annual 
reports of telecommunications 
providers such as PT Telekomunikasi 
Indonesia (Telkom), PT Telkomsel, PT 
Indosat, and others.
b. Processing data from surveys that 
have been conducted by BPS, including 
the National Socio-Economic Survey 
(Susenas) 2019-2024, as well as the 
Village Potential Data Collection (Podes) 
2024.
c. Studying national and international 
journals and papers on communication

C. KONSEP DAN DEFINISI
1. Bandwidth adalah kapasitas dari 
suatu jaringan.
2. Base Tranceiver Station  (BTS) 
adalah komponen jaringan dari 
sistem komunikasi mobile yang 
menerima dan mengirim sinyal. BTS 
berfungsi menjembatani perangkat 
komunikasi pengguna dengan 
jaringan menuju jaringan lain.
3. Data Potensi Desa  (Podes) adalah 
data hasil pendataan Podes yang 
mengandung informasi potensi dan 
kinerja pembangunan di tingkat 
desa/ kelurahan berupa indikator 
sosial ekonomi, sarana prasarana, 
serta potensi desa lainnya. Data 
hasil pendataan Podes hingga saat 
ini merupakan satu-satunya sumber 
data tematik berbasis wilayah yang 
mampu menggambarkan potensi 
suatu wilayah setingkat desa di 
seluruh Indonesia.
4. Data Survei Sosial Ekonomi 
Nasional (Susenas) merupakan 
data hasil survei rumah tangga 
yang dilaksanakan oleh Badan 
Pusat Statistik, berisikan berbagai 
informasi kependudukan, 
pendidikan, kesehatan, perumahan, 
kriminalitas, sosial budaya, 
konsumsi, perjalanan wisata dan 
kesejahteraan rumah tangga.
5. GSM adalah standar Eropa untuk 
telepon Seluler digital yang 
memiliki kemampuan untuk 
international satelite roaming. 
Hal ini memberikan sebuah 
and information technology to enrich 
this publication.
C. CONCEPT AND DEFINITION
1. Bandwidth is the capacity of a 
network.
2. Base Transceiver Station  (BTS) is 
a network component of cellular 
communication systems that receive 
and send signals. BTS serves to 
bridge the users’ communication 
device with the network to other 
networks.
3.  Village Potential Data Collection  
(the Podes) is the result of the Podes’ 
data collection, which contains 
information on potential and 
performance development at the 
village/urban village such as socio 
economic indicators, infrastructure,  
and other villages potentials. The 
Podes data collection results until 
today are the only source of thematic 
data based on areas that are able 
to describe the potential of village-
level areas throughout Indonesia.
4. National Socioeconomic Survey  
(the Susenas) Data is household 
survey data conducted by BPS - 
Statistics Indonesia. It contains 
various information on population, 
education, health, housing, crime, 
social culture, consumption, travel, 
and household welfare.
5. GSM is the European standard for 
digital mobile/cellular phones that 
can help international satellite 
roaming. It provides a limitless 
system that can connect more than

159 countries and provide services to 
remote areas.
6. Telecommunication  
Manufacturing Industry  is an 
industry that produces finished/
semi-finished goods or components 
to use in telecommunications such 
as: components and electronic 
boards, computers and accessories, 
communication equipment, audio 
equipment and video electronics, 
and magnetic media and optical 
media (ISIC: 2610, 2620, 2630, 2640 
dan 2680).
7. Interconnected Network (Internet) 
is a global communication network 
system that connects computers and 
computer network around the world 
globally. The facility provides access 
to a number of communication 
services, including world wide web 
(www), electronic mail (email), 
news, entertainment, and data. 
The internet access facility is not 
only through computers, but also 
cellular phones, PDA, electronic 
game devices, digital television, etc. 
to access the internet, it can be done 
via a fixed network or cellular.
8. Internet Service Provider  (ISP) 
is a company or agency which 
has internet connection services 
and other related services. Most 
telephone companies are internet 
service providers. The company 
provides services such as connection 
to the internet, domain name 
registration and hosting.

6. Industri Manufaktur 
Telekomunikasi adalah industri 
yang menghasilkan barang jadi/
setengah jadi atau komponen 
barang untuk digunakan dalam

komunikasi, peralatan audio

magnetik dan media optik (KBLI: 
2610, 2620, 2630, 2640 dan 2680).
7. Interconnected Network 
(Internet) adalah sebuah sistem 
jaringan komunikasi global yang 
menghubungkan komputer dan 
jaringan komputer di seluruh 
dunia secara global. Fasilitas 
menyediakan akses ke sejumlah

hiburan dan data. Fasilitas akses 
internet tidak diasumsikan hanya 
melalui komputer, dimungkinkan 
juga menggunakan telepon Seluler, 
PDA, perangkat game elektronik, 
televisi digital, dan lain-lain. Akses 
bisa melalui suatu jaringan tetap 
maupun Seluler.

Penyelenggara Jasa Internet (P JI)

internet dan jasa lainnya yang 
berhubungan. Kebanyakan 
perusahaan telepon merupakan 
penyelenggara jasa internet.

Catatan Teknis/Technical Notes
9. The Telecommunication Network  
is a series of telecommunication 
tools and its complement 
used for telecommunication 
activities. Telecommunication 
network activities cover fixed 
network provision for public 
telecommunications and leased 
circuits and operation of cellular 
telecommunication networks 
provision such as terrestrial mobile 
networks, cellular, and satellites.
10. Telecommunication Services  
are served to satisfy the needs of 
telecommunication used network. 
Telecommunication services 
include the activities which 
provide basic telephony services 
(telephone, telegraph, telex, and 
facsimile), value-added telephone 
services (intelligent network 
services, calling cards, interactive 
technology services, and public 
radio call), multimedia services 
(VoIP , internet, data communication, 
video conferencing, and video 
entertainment services).
11. Network Access Point  (NAP) is a 
company that can formally serve 
the needs and sell bandwidth to 
ISPs based on the permissions from 
the Ministry of Communication and 
Digital.
12. Telecommunications Subscribers 
refer to individuals, legal entities, 
and government agencies that 
use, or utilize telecommunication 
jasa seperti hubungan ke internet, 
pendaftaran nama domain dan 
hosting.
9. Jaringan Telekomunikasi 
merupakan rangkaian 
perangkat telekomunikasi dan 
kelengkapannya yang digunakan 
untuk kegiatan telekomunikasi. 
Termasuk dalam kegiatan jaringan 
telekomunikasi adalah kegiatan 
penyelenggaraan jaringan tetap 
untuk telekomunikasi publik dan 
sirkuit sewa, serta penyelenggaraan 
jaringan telekomunikasi bergerak 
seperti jaringan bergerak terestrial, 
Seluler dan satelit.
10. Jasa Telekomunikasi  adalah 
layanan untuk memenuhi 
kebutuhan telekomunikasi 
dengan menggunakan 
jaringan telekomunikasi. Jasa 
tekomunikasi mencakup kegiatan 
penyelenggaraan jasa telepon 
dasar (telepon, telegrap, teleks, dan 
faksimili), jasa nilai tambah telepon 
(jasa jaringan pintar, kartu panggil, 
jasa-jasa teknologi interaktif, 
dan radio panggil untuk umum), 
jasa multimedia (VoIP , internet, 
komunikasi data, konferensi video, 
dan jasa video hiburan).
11. Network Access Point  (NAP) 
adalah perusahaan yang secara 
resmi dapat melayani kebutuhan 
sekaligus menjual bandwidth 
kepada ISP berdasarkan perizinan 
dari Kementerian Komunikasi dan 
Digital.

merujuk kepada perseorangan, 
badan hukum, dan instansi 
pemerintah yang menggunakan

conducting telecommunication 
networks, telecommunication

from the Ministry of Communication

14. Telecommunication implies every 
transmission, delivery, and admission 
of every information by signs, 
signals, writings, images, voices and 
sounds through a wire, optical, radio, 
or other electromagnetic systems. 
Telecommunication activities 
include the provision of voice 
transmitters, data, text, sound and 
video using transmission facilities 
based on either single technology or 
combination of various technologies.
15. Line Telecommunication  is 
activity of operation, maintenance, 
or providing access to facilities 
for voice transmission, data, text, 
sound, and video by using line 
telecommunication infrastructures. 
It includes data communication 
connection activities delivered by a 
package, through a central or other 
networks such as Public Switch 
Telephone Networks (PSTN). It also 
includes the terrestrial network 
implementation activities that serve 
certain cellular Subscribers such 
as trunked radio services and other 
services for the public radio call.

kombinasi dari berbagai teknologi.

tertentu seperti jasa radio trunking

16. Wireless Telecommunication  is 
a network operation activity that 
serves cellular telecommunications 
with cellular technology at the 
earth’s surface. Its activities are 
the operation, maintenance, or 
supplying access to facilities for 
the transmission of voices, data, 
texts, sounds, and video by using 
wireless and cellular communication 
infrastructures and operations and 
maintenances of a call number 
(paging), as well as other wireless 
telecommunications.
17. Fixed Line Telephone or a home 
telephone is a telecommunication 
network using fixed devices with 
a line that is generally set by 
technical standards by using a 
telephone number, also known as 
Public Switch Telephone Network 
(PSTN). Generally, it is used for home 
telephone and internet networks, it 
has the ability to deliver a strong and 
clear signal with relatively cheaper 
cost.
18. Fixed Wireless Access  (FWA) refers 
to the local wireless transmission 
network using cellular technology, 
micro or radio waves to connect a 
Subscriber at a fixed location to a 
local exchange. FWA licenses use 
CDMA (Code Division Multiple Access) 
technologies that follow the regular 
telephone number with area code 
that cannot be carried out from the 
area, except by replacing with local 
dan jasa radio panggil untuk umum.

bunyi dan video menggunakan

kabel dan telekomunikasi 
Seluler dan pemeliharaan dan

(paging), seperti halnya jaringan
